decisions\\n(fill)', '## decisions\\nkeep the cache').replace('## next_action\\n(fill)', '## next_action\\nrun the bench'));\n  hook(HANDOFF, { hook_event_name: 'SessionEnd', session_id: 's1', cwd: root }, cfg);\n  const again = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');\n  ok('a regenerated handoff keeps what the model wrote', /keep the cache/.test(again) && /run the bench/.test(again) && /## task\\nadd a === '');\n\n  const resume = hook(COUNT, { hook_event_name: 'SessionStart', source: 'resume', session_id: 's1', cwd: root }, cfg);\n  ok('the next session start points at the handoff', =>\n512\t    run(process.execPath, [LOOP], { cwd: CORE, input: JSON.stringify({ tool_name: tool, tool_input: { command } }), env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: home() } });\n513\t  const deny = (r) => {\n514\t    try {\n515\t      return JSON.parse(r.stdout).hookSpecificOutput.permissionDecision === 'deny';\n516\t    } catch {\n517\t      return false;\n518\t    }\n519\t  };\n520\t  const forever = call('Bash', 'until grep -qE \"passed|failed\" out.txt; do sleep 5; done; tail -4 out.txt');\n521\t  ok('an until loop with no bound is denied', forever.status === 0 && deny(forever), forever.stdout);\n522\t  ok('the denial explains how to bound it', /timeout/.test(forever.stdout), forever.stdout);\n523\t  ok('a while true loop is denied too', deny(call('Bash', 'while true; do sleep 10; done')));\n524\t  ok('a PowerShell wait loop is denied', deny(call('PowerShell', 'while (-not (Test-Path out.txt)) { Start-Sleep 5 }')));\n525\t  ok('timeout makes it pass', !deny(call('Bash', 'timeout 5400 bash -c \"until grep -q passed out.txt; do sleep 5; done\"')));\n526\t  ok('a counter makes it pass', !deny(call('Bash', 'i=0; until grep -q passed out.txt || [ $i -ge 60 ]; do sleep 5; i=$((i+1)); done')));\n527\t  ok('a Get-Date deadline makes it pass', !deny(call('PowerShell', '$end = (Get-Date).AddMinutes(90); while ((Get-Date) -lt $end -and -not (Test-Path !deny(call('Bash', 'npm test --silent && git status')));\n530\t  ok('a Write is not looked at', !deny(call('Write', 'until x; do sleep 1; done')));\n531\t  const junk = run(process.execPath, [LOOP], { =>\n512\t    run(process.execPath, [LOOP], { cwd: CORE, input: JSON.stringify({ tool_name: tool, tool_input: { command } }), env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: home() } });\n513\t  const deny = (r) => {\n514\t    try {\n515\t      return JSON.parse(r.stdout).hookSpecificOutput.permissionDecision === 'deny';\n516\t    } catch {\n517\t      return false;\n518\t    }\n519\t  };\n520\t  const forever = call('Bash', 'until grep -qE \"passed|failed\" out.txt; do sleep 5; done; tail -4 out.txt');\n521\t  ok('an until loop with no bound is denied', forever.status === 0 && deny(forever), forever.stdout);\n522\t  ok('the denial explains how to bound it', /timeout/.test(forever.stdout), forever.stdout);\n523\t  ok('a while true loop is denied too', deny(call('Bash', 'while true; do sleep 10; done')));\n524\t  ok('a PowerShell wait loop is denied', deny(call('PowerShell', 'while (-not (Test-Path out.txt)) { Start-Sleep 5 }')));\n525\t  ok('timeout makes it pass', !deny(call('Bash', 'timeout 5400 bash -c \"until grep -q passed out.txt; do sleep 5; done\"')));\n526\t  ok('a counter makes it pass', !deny(call('Bash', 'i=0; until grep -q passed out.txt || [ $i -ge 60 ]; do sleep 5; i=$((i+1)); done')));\n527\t  ok('a Get-Date deadline makes it pass', !deny(call('PowerShell', '$end = (Get-Date).AddMinutes(90); while ((Get-Date) -lt $end -and -not (Test-Path !deny(call('Bash', 'npm test --silent && git status')));\n530\t  ok('a Write is not looked at', !deny(call('Write', 'until x; do sleep 1; done')));\n531\t  const junk = run(process.execPath, [LOOP], { =>\n512\t    run(process.execPath, [LOOP], { cwd: CORE, input: JSON.stringify({ tool_name: tool, tool_input: { command } }), env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: home() } });\n513\t  const deny = (r) => {\n514\t    try {\n515\t      return JSON.parse(r.stdout).hookSpecificOutput.permissionDecision === 'deny';\n516\t    } catch {\n517\t      return false;\n518\t    }\n519\t  };\n520\t  const forever = call('Bash', 'until grep -qE \"passed|failed\" out.txt; do sleep 5; done; tail -4 out.txt');\n521\t  ok('an until loop with no bound is denied', forever.status === 0 && deny(forever),
